Reviewers requested changes. Address every single comment in the reviews below by editing code in

## Project context
ATRIA-X: multi-tenant SaaS for home-care agencies. React19+Vite8+TS, Tailwind4, React Router 7, Clerk (auth/orgs), Convex (backend+DB), Vitest, Playwright. Formatting: 2-space indent, single quotes, NO semicolons, PascalCase components, camelCase utils. CORE RULE: a service is never billed unless its shift documentation is complete and approved (caregiver->coordinator->billing). Multi-tenancy is the central invariant: every Convex fn touching tenant data MUST go through authHelpers.ts guards (requireTenant / requireTenantRole / assertTenantDoc); enforce auth twice (RouteGuard + server). Roles: org:admin, org:coordinator, org:caregiver. After editing convex/* run `npx convex codegen`. Never edit convex/_generated by hand. Follow CLAUDE.md/AGENTS.md.

## Original task
ATRIA-X SESSION 4 — Minimal caregiver identity + ADP sync + geofence settings UI

Run this through the ATRIA-X multi-model pipeline using codex + kimi only: plan/review via Codex CLI, implementation/fix via Kimi CLI. Opus/Claude must not be used.

Repo: C:\Users\pinol\Documents\Work\atriax\solid-pancake

Follow atriax-orchestrator and atriax-rules:
- All tenant data through convex/authHelpers.ts
- requireTenantRole for server-side authorization
- Clerk organization roles: org:admin, org:coordinator, org:caregiver
- npx convex codegen after Convex edits
- Style: single quotes, no semicolons, 2-space indent
- Do not build candidate application or HR onboarding
- Keep this Phase 1 backend + thin admin/coordinator UI minimal

Task:

ATRIA-X Phase 1 backend + thin admin/coordinator UI for minimal caregiver identity and geofence settings, reusing convex/members.ts, convex/invitations.ts, employeeProfiles, tenant shiftGeofence settings, and the ADP sync engine.

Required implementation:

1. Backend mutation createCaregiver({ displayName, email })
   - requireTenantRole org:admin
   - Creates or updates an employeeProfiles row
   - Sends the existing Clerk invitation so the caregiver can log in, reusing convex/invitations.ts
   - Enqueues adpSyncWorker so the new caregiver is reflected in ADP when configured
   - If ADP credentials/config are missing, queue/mark as pending_credentials until then
   - On invite acceptance, link employeeProfiles.clerkUserId + tenantMemberId

2. Backend admin mutation runAdpInitialWorkerLoad()
   - requireTenantRole org:admin
   - Schedules/runs adpInitialWorkerLoad from the prior session
   - Returns a summary with matched/created/errors counts

3. Backend query listEmployeeProfiles
   - Tenant-guarded
   - Admin-facing
   - Return displayName, email, role, adpSyncStatus

4. Thin admin UI under src/features/team
   - Reuse existing Team page patterns and src/shared/ui primitives
   - Role-gated through RouteGuard/TenantRoleRouteGuard as appropriate
   - Add a "Caregivers & ADP sync" panel
   - List employees with adpSyncStatus pill, with color + WORD:
     - Synced = green
     - Queued = amber
     - Pending credentials = gray
     - Error = red
   - Add caregiver form with name + email fields
   - "Load from ADP" button calls runAdpInitialWorkerLoad and shows a result toast

5. Compact "Clock-in location rules" settings panel
   - Visible to admins/coordinators
   - Enable/disable geofence
   - Enforce on clock-in
   - Enforce on clock-out
   - Default radius meters
   - Max accuracy meters
   - Plain-language help text
   - Editable by admin/coordinator
   - Rejected for caregiver on backend

6. Client/shift location editing where it naturally fits
   - Client record or shift detail, whichever matches existing patterns best
   - Service address + latitude/longitude fields, or saved geocoded target
   - If no coordinates are configured and geofence is enabled, warn that clock-in/out will be blocked until an address is completed

Tests required:
- createCaregiver enqueues a worker sync + creates a profile
- initial-load summary counts are correct with mocked ADP
- geofence settings are editable by admin/coordinator and rejected for caregiver

Required gates:
- npx convex codegen
- npm run lint
- npm run typecheck
- npm run test

Quality/scope constraints:
- Minimal Phase 1 only
- Do not build candidate/HR onboarding
- Preserve existing flows unless tests prove the intended change
- Do not claim success unless codegen/lint/typecheck/tests required by this session are actually green

Optimization guidance:
- Avoid wasting loops on formatting-only churn; run the required focused tests and gates promptly after implementation
- Keep scope tight to the listed files/features
- Do not add broad refactors or unrelated UI redesigns

act(() => {
  /* fire events that update state */

@@ -1,15 +1,341 @@
 import { v } from 'convex/values'
-import { internalAction } from './_generated/server'
+import {
+  internalMutation,
+  internalQuery,
+  mutation,
+  type MutationCtx,
+  type QueryCtx,
+} from './_generated/server'
+import { internal } from './_generated/api'
+import type { Id } from './_generated/dataModel'
+import { assertTenantDoc, requireTenantRole } from './authHelpers'
+import { adpEnvVarNames, isAdpConfigured } from './integrations/adp/config'
 
-export const adpSyncPunch = internalAction({
-  args: { punchId: v.id('timePunches') },
-  handler: async (_ctx, { punchId }) => {
-    // Stub: ADP credentials are not configured yet. When credentials are
-    // available this action will queue the punch to ADP Workforce Now.
-    return {
-      status: 'skipped',
-      reason: 'ADP credentials not configured',
-      punchId: punchId as string,
+type ClaimResult =
+  | {
+      type: 'already_synced'
+      eventId: Id<'integrationEvents'>
+      response?: unknown
     }
+  | { type: 'in_flight'; eventId: Id<'integrationEvents'> }
+  | { type: 'max_attempts'; attempt: number }
+  | { type: 'claimed'; eventId: Id<'integrationEvents'>; attempt: number }
+
+const RETRY_DELAYS_MS = [30_000, 120_000, 300_000, 900_000, 1_800_000]
+const IN_FLIGHT_STALE_MS = 5 * 60 * 1000
+
+const adpPunchSyncStatus = v.union(
+  v.literal('pending_credentials'),
+  v.literal('queued'),
+  v.literal('synced'),
+  v.literal('error'),
+)
+
+const adpEmployeeSyncStatus = v.union(
+  v.literal('pending_credentials'),
+  v.literal('queued'),
+  v.literal('synced'),
+  v.literal('error'),
+  v.literal('matched'),
+  v.literal('created'),
+)
+
+export function sanitizeError(error: unknown): string {
+  const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)
+  return redactSecrets(message.slice(0, 500))
+}
+
+function redactSecrets(message: string): string {
+  let redacted = message
+
+  for (const name of adpEnvVarNames()) {
+    const value = process.env[name]?.trim()
+    if (value) {
+      redacted = redacted.split(value).join(`[REDACTED:${name}]`)
+    }
+  }
+
+  redacted = redacted.replace(
+    /Basic\s+[A-Za-z0-9+/=]{10,}/g,
+    'Basic [REDACTED]',
+  )
+  redacted = redacted.replace(
+    /Bearer\s+[A-Za-z0-9_\-./=]{10,}/g,
+    'Bearer [REDACTED]',
+  )
+  redacted = redacted.replace(
+    /(token['"]?\s*[:=]\s*)['"]?[A-Za-z0-9_\-./=]{8,}['"]?/gi,
+    '$1[REDACTED]',
+  )
+
+  return redacted
+}
+
+export function normalizeEmail(email: string): string {
+  return email.trim().toLowerCase()
+}
+
+export function idempotencyKey(kind: string, refId: string): string {
+  return `${kind}:${refId}`
+}
+
+export function chooseRetryDelayMs(attempt: number): number {
+  return RETRY_DELAYS_MS[Math.min(attempt, RETRY_DELAYS_MS.length) - 1]
+}
+
+export const loadPunchForAdpSync = internalQuery({
+  args: { timePunchId: v.id('timePunches') },
+  handler: async (ctx: QueryCtx, { timePunchId }) => {
+    const punch = await ctx.db.get(timePunchId)
+    if (!punch) throw new Error('Time punch not found.')
+    return punch
   },
 })
+
+export const loadEmployeeProfileForAdpSync = internalQuery({
+  args: { employeeProfileId: v.id('employeeProfiles') },
+  handler: async (ctx: QueryCtx, { employeeProfileId }) => {
+    const profile = await ctx.db.get(employeeProfileId)
+    if (!profile) throw new Error('Employee profile not found.')
+    return profile
+  },
+})
+
+export const findEmployeeProfileByClerkUserId = internalQuery({
+  args: { tenantId: v.id('tenants'), clerkUserId: v.string() },
+  handler: async (ctx: QueryCtx, { tenantId, clerkUserId }) => {
+    return ctx.db
+      .query('employeeProfiles')
+      .withIndex('by_tenant_clerk_user', (q) =>
+        q.eq('tenantId', tenantId).eq('clerkUserId', clerkUserId),
+      )
+      .unique()
+  },
+})
+
+export const findEmployeeProfilesByTenant = internalQuery({
+  args: { tenantId: v.id('tenants') },
+  handler: async (ctx: QueryCtx, { tenantId }) => {
+    return ctx.db
+      .query('employeeProfiles')
+      .withIndex('by_tenant', (q) => q.eq('tenantId', tenantId))
+      .collect()
+  },
+})
+
+export const isAdpConfiguredForTenant = internalQuery({
+  args: { tenantId: v.id('tenants') },
+  handler: async (ctx: QueryCtx, { tenantId }) => {
+    return isAdpConfigured(ctx, tenantId)
+  },
+})
+
+export const patchPunchAdpStatus = internalMutation({
+  args: {
+    timePunchId: v.id('timePunches'),
+    tenantId: v.id('tenants'),
+    adpSyncStatus: adpPunchSyncStatus,
+    adpPunchId: v.optional(v.string()),
+    adpError: v.optional(v.string()),
+  },
+  handler: async (ctx: MutationCtx, args) => {
+    const punch = await ctx.db.get(args.timePunchId)
+    if (!punch) throw new Error('Time punch not found.')
+    assertTenantDoc(punch, args.tenantId)
+
+    const patch: {
+      adpSyncStatus: 'pending_credentials' | 'queued' | 'synced' | 'error'
+      adpPunchId?: string
+      adpError?: string
+    } = { adpSyncStatus: args.adpSyncStatus }
+    if (args.adpPunchId !== undefined) patch.adpPunchId = args.adpPunchId
+    if (args.adpError !== undefined || args.adpSyncStatus === 'synced') {
+      patch.adpError = args.adpError
+    }
+    await ctx.db.patch(args.timePunchId, patch)
+  },
+})
+
+export const patchWorkerAdpStatus = internalMutation({
+  args: {
+    employeeProfileId: v.id('employeeProfiles'),
+    tenantId: v.id('tenants'),
+    adpSyncStatus: adpEmployeeSyncStatus,
+    adpAssociateOid: v.optional(v.string()),
+    adpWorkerId: v.optional(v.string()),
+    adpError: v.optional(v.string()),
+  },
+  handler: async (ctx: MutationCtx, args) => {
+    const profile = await ctx.db.get(args.employeeProfileId)
+    if (!profile) throw new Error('Employee profile not found.')
+    assertTenantDoc(profile, args.tenantId)
+
+    const isSuccessStatus =
+      args.adpSyncStatus === 'synced' ||
+      args.adpSyncStatus === 'matched' ||
+      args.adpSyncStatus === 'created'
+
+    const patch: {
+      adpSyncStatus:
+        | 'pending_credentials'
+        | 'queued'
+        | 'synced'
+        | 'error'
+        | 'matched'
+        | 'created'
+      adpAssociateOid?: string
+      adpWorkerId?: string
+      adpError?: string
+    } = { adpSyncStatus: args.adpSyncStatus }
+    if (args.adpAssociateOid !== undefined) {
+      patch.adpAssociateOid = args.adpAssociateOid
+    }
+    if (args.adpWorkerId !== undefined) patch.adpWorkerId = args.adpWorkerId
+    if (args.adpError !== undefined || isSuccessStatus) {
+      patch.adpError = args.adpError
+    }
+    await ctx.db.patch(args.employeeProfileId, patch)
+  },
+})
+
+export const upsertMatchedEmployeeProfile = internalMutation({
+  args: {
+    tenantId: v.id('tenants'),
+    associateOID: v.string(),
+    workerID: v.optional(v.string()),
+    displayName: v.string(),
+    email: v.string(),
+    matchProfileId: v.optional(v.id('employeeProfiles')),
+  },
+  handler: async (ctx: MutationCtx, args) => {
+    if (args.matchProfileId) {
+      const match = await ctx.db.get(args.matchProfileId)
+      if (!match) throw new Error('Employee profile not found.')
+      assertTenantDoc(match, args.tenantId)
+      await ctx.db.patch(args.matchProfileId, {
+        adpAssociateOid: args.associateOID,
+        adpWorkerId: args.workerID,
+        adpSyncStatus: 'matched',
+      })
+      return args.matchProfileId
+    }
+
+    return ctx.db.insert('employeeProfiles', {
+      tenantId: args.tenantId,
+      displayName: args.displayName,
+      email: args.email,
+      adpAssociateOid: args.associateOID,
+      adpWorkerId: args.workerID,
+      adpSyncStatus: 'created',
+      createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
+    })
+  },
+})
+
+export const claimIntegrationEvent = internalMutation({
+  args: {
+    tenantId: v.id('tenants'),
+    kind: v.string(),
+    refId: v.string(),
+    idempotencyKey: v.string(),
+    request: v.optional(v.any()),
+    maxAttempts: v.number(),
+  },
+  handler: async (ctx: MutationCtx, args): Promise<ClaimResult> => {
+    const events = await ctx.db
+      .query('integrationEvents')
+      .withIndex('by_tenant_idemp', (q) =>
+        q.eq('tenantId', args.tenantId).eq('idempotencyKey', args.idempotencyKey),
+      )
+      .order('desc')
+      .collect()
+
+    for (const event of events) {
+      if (event.status === 'success') {
+        return {
+          type: 'already_synced',
+          eventId: event._id,
+          response: event.response,
+        }
+      }
+      if (event.status === 'attempting') {
+        const createdAt = new Date(event.createdAt).getTime()
+        if (Date.now() - createdAt < IN_FLIGHT_STALE_MS) {
+          return { type: 'in_flight', eventId: event._id }
+        }
+      }
+    }
+
+    const latestAttempt = events.find((e) => typeof e.attempt === 'number')
+    const attempt = (latestAttempt?.attempt ?? 0) + 1
+    if (attempt > args.maxAttempts) {
+      return { type: 'max_attempts', attempt }
+    }
+
+    const eventId = await ctx.db.insert('integrationEvents', {
+      tenantId: args.tenantId,
+      provider: 'adp',
+      kind: args.kind,
+      refId: args.refId,
+      idempotencyKey: args.idempotencyKey,
+      status: 'attempting',
+      attempt,
+      request: args.request,
+      createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
+    })
+
+    return { type: 'claimed', eventId, attempt }
+  },
+})
+
+export const patchIntegrationEvent = internalMutation({
+  args: {
+    eventId: v.id('integrationEvents'),
+    status: v.string(),
+    response: v.optional(v.any()),
+    completedAt: v.optional(v.string()),
+    nextRetryAt: v.optional(v.string()),
+  },
+  handler: async (ctx: MutationCtx, args) => {
+    await ctx.db.patch(args.eventId, {
+      status: args.status,
+      response: args.response,
+      completedAt: args.completedAt,
+      nextRetryAt: args.nextRetryAt,
+    })
+  },
+})
+
+export const triggerInitialWorkerLoad = mutation({
+  args: { clerkOrgId: v.string() },
+  handler: async (ctx, { clerkOrgId }) => {
+    const { tenantId } = await requireTenantRole(ctx, clerkOrgId, [
+      'org:admin',
+    ])
+    await ctx.scheduler.runAfter(
+      0,
+      internal.adpOutbound.adpInitialWorkerLoad,
+      { tenantId },
+    )
+    return { status: 'queued' }
+  },
+})
+
+export const adpSyncStatusLiterals = {
+  adpPunchSyncStatus,
+  adpEmployeeSyncStatus,
+}
+
+export type AdpPunchSyncStatus =
+  | 'pending_credentials'
+  | 'queued'
+  | 'synced'
+  | 'error'
+
+export type AdpEmployeeSyncStatus =
+  | 'pending_credentials'
+  | 'queued'
+  | 'synced'
+  | 'error'
+  | 'matched'
+  | 'created'

+export async function sendClerkInvitation(args: {
+  secretKey: string
+  inviterUserId: string
+  clerkOrgId: string
+  emailAddress: string
+  role: InviteRole
+  appBaseUrl: string
+}) {
+  const response = await fetch(
+    `https://api.clerk.com/v1/organizations/${args.clerkOrgId}/invitations`,
+    {
+      method: 'POST',
+      headers: {
+        Authorization: `Bearer ${args.secretKey}`,
+        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
+      },
+      body: JSON.stringify({
+        inviter_user_id: args.inviterUserId,
+        email_address: args.emailAddress.trim(),
+        role: toClerkRole(args.role),
+        redirect_url: invitationRedirectUrl(args.appBaseUrl),
+        public_metadata: { atriaRole: args.role },
+      }),
+    },
+  )
+
+  const payload = await response.json()
+  if (!response.ok) {
+    throw new ConvexError(clerkErrorMessage(payload))
+  }
+
+  const record = payload as Record<string, unknown>
+  return {
+    id: String(record.id ?? ''),
+    emailAddress: String(record.email_address ?? args.emailAddress),
+    role: String(record.role ?? toClerkRole(args.role)),
+    roleName: String(record.role_name ?? toClerkRole(args.role)),
+    status: String(record.status ?? 'pending'),
+    createdAt: normalizeCreatedAt(record.created_at),
+  }
+}
+

 113: export const createCaregiver = action({
 114:   args: {
 115:     clerkOrgId: v.string(),
 116:     displayName: v.string(),
 117:     email: v.string(),
 118:     appBaseUrl: v.string(),
 119:   },
 120:   handler: async (
 121:     ctx,
 122:     args,
 123:   ): Promise<{
 124:     profileId: Id<'employeeProfiles'>
 125:     invitationId: string
 126:     emailAddress: string
 127:     role: string
 128:     status: string
 129:   }> => {
 130:     const identity = await ctx.auth.getUserIdentity()
 131:     if (!identity) {
 132:       throw new Error('Unauthorized: authentication required.')
 133:     }
 134: 
 135:     const tenant = await ctx.runQuery(api.tenants.get, {
 136:       clerkOrgId: args.clerkOrgId,
 137:     })
 138:     if (!tenant) {
 139:       throw new Error('Tenant not found.')
 140:     }
 141:     if (tenant.role !== 'org:admin') {
 142:       throw new Error('Forbidden: only agency admins can create caregivers.')
 143:     }
 144: 
 145:     const secretKey = process.env.CLERK_SECRET_KEY
 146:     if (!secretKey) {
 147:       throw new Error('Server invitation configuration is missing CLERK_SECRET_KEY.')
 148:     }
 149: 
 150:     const invitation = await sendClerkInvitation({
 151:       secretKey,
 152:       inviterUserId: identity.subject,
 153:       clerkOrgId: args.clerkOrgId,
 154:       emailAddress: args.email,
 155:       role: 'org:caregiver',
 156:       appBaseUrl: args.appBaseUrl,
 157:     })
 158: 
 159:     const profileId: Id<'employeeProfiles'> = await ctx.runMutation(
 160:       internal.employeeProfiles.createCaregiverProfile,
 161:       {
 162:         tenantId: tenant._id,
 163:         displayName: args.displayName,
 164:         email: args.email,
 165:       },
 166:     )
 167: 
 168:     return {
 169:       profileId,
 170:       invitationId: invitation.id,
 171:       emailAddress: invitation.emailAddress,
 172:       role: invitation.role,
 173:       status: invitation.status,
 174:     }
 175:   },
 176: })

  45: export const adpSyncPunch = internalAction({
  46:   args: { timePunchId: v.id('timePunches') },
  47:   handler: async (ctx: ActionCtx, { timePunchId }) => {
  48:     const punch = await ctx.runQuery(internal.adpSync.loadPunchForAdpSync, {
  49:       timePunchId,
  50:     })
  51:     const tenantId = punch.tenantId
  52: 
  53:     const configured = await ctx.runQuery(
  54:       internal.adpSync.isAdpConfiguredForTenant,
  55:       { tenantId },
  56:     )
  57:     if (!configured) {
  58:       await ctx.runMutation(internal.adpSync.patchPunchAdpStatus, {
  59:         timePunchId,
  60:         tenantId,
  61:         adpSyncStatus: 'pending_credentials',
  62:       })
  63:       return { status: 'pending_credentials' }
  64:     }
  65: 
  66:     const profile = await ctx.runQuery(
  67:       internal.adpSync.findEmployeeProfileByClerkUserId,
  68:       { tenantId, clerkUserId: punch.caregiverId },
  69:     )
  70: 
  71:     if (!profile || !profile.adpAssociateOid) {
  72:       await ctx.runMutation(internal.adpSync.patchPunchAdpStatus, {
  73:         timePunchId,
  74:         tenantId,
  75:         adpSyncStatus: 'error',
  76:         adpError: 'Caregiver employee profile or ADP associate OID missing.',
  77:       })
  78:       return { status: 'error' }
  79:     }
  80: 
  81:     const key = idempotencyKey('punch', timePunchId as string)
  82:     const claim = await ctx.runMutation(
  83:       internal.adpSync.claimIntegrationEvent,
  84:       {
  85:         tenantId,
  86:         kind: 'punch',
  87:         refId: timePunchId as string,
  88:         idempotencyKey: key,
  89:         request: {
  90:           timePunchId: timePunchId as string,
  91:           punchType: punch.punchType,
  92:           associateOID: profile.adpAssociateOid,
  93:         },
  94:         maxAttempts: MAX_ATTEMPTS,
  95:       },
  96:     )
  97: 
  98:     if (claim.type === 'already_synced') {
  99:       await ctx.runMutation(internal.adpSync.patchPunchAdpStatus, {
 100:         timePunchId,
 101:         tenantId,
 102:         adpSyncStatus: 'synced',
 103:       })
 104:       return { status: 'already_synced' }
 105:     }
 106: 
 107:     if (claim.type === 'in_flight') {
 108:       return { status: 'in_flight' }
 109:     }
 110: 
 111:     if (claim.type === 'max_attempts') {
 112:       await ctx.runMutation(internal.adpSync.patchPunchAdpStatus, {
 113:         timePunchId,
 114:         tenantId,
 115:         adpSyncStatus: 'error',
 116:         adpError: 'Max ADP sync attempts exceeded.',
 117:       })
 118:       return { status: 'max_attempts' }
 119:     }
 120: 
 121:     try {
 122:       const port = await getAdpPort()
 123:       const result = await port.postPunch({
 124:         idempotencyKey: key,
 125:         timePunchId: timePunchId as string,
 126:         associateOID: profile.adpAssociateOid,
 127:         punchType: punch.punchType,
 128:         at: punch.at,
 129:       })
 130: 
 131:       await ctx.runMutation(internal.adpSync.patchPunchAdpStatus, {
 132:         timePunchId,
 133:         tenantId,
 134:         adpSyncStatus: 'synced',
 135:         adpPunchId: result.punchId,
 136:       })
 137: 
 138:       await patchEvent(ctx, claim.eventId, {
 139:         status: 'success',
 140:         response: { adpPunchId: result.punchId },
 141:         completedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
 142:       })
 143: 
 144:       return { status: 'synced', adpPunchId: result.punchId }
 145:     } catch (error) {
 146:       const isNotConfigured = error instanceof NotConfiguredError
 147:       const syncStatus = isNotConfigured ? 'pending_credentials' : 'error'
 148:       const adpError = sanitizeError(error)
 149:       const nextRetryAt =
 150:         !isNotConfigured && claim.attempt < MAX_ATTEMPTS
 151:           ? new Date(
 152:               Date.now() + chooseRetryDelayMs(claim.attempt),
 153:             ).toISOString()
 154:           : undefined
 155: 
 156:       await patchEvent(ctx, claim.eventId, {
 157:         status: syncStatus,
 158:         response: { error: adpError },
 159:         completedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
 160:         nextRetryAt,
 161:       })
 162: 
 163:       await ctx.runMutation(internal.adpSync.patchPunchAdpStatus, {
 164:         timePunchId,
 165:         tenantId,
 166:         adpSyncStatus: syncStatus,
 167:         adpError,
 168:       })
 169: 
 170:       if (!isNotConfigured && claim.attempt < MAX_ATTEMPTS) {
 171:         await ctx.scheduler.runAfter(
 172:           chooseRetryDelayMs(claim.attempt),
 173:           internal.adpOutbound.adpSyncPunch,
 174:           { timePunchId },
 175:         )
 176:       }
 177: 
 178:       return { status: syncStatus, error: adpError }
 179:     }
 180:   },
 181: })

 254:     }
 255: 
 256:     try {
 257:       const port = await getAdpPort()
 258:       const result = await port.createWorker({
 259:         idempotencyKey: key,
 260:         employeeProfileId: employeeProfileId as string,
 261:         displayName: profile.displayName,
 262:         email: profile.email,
 263:       })
 264: 
 265:       await ctx.runMutation(internal.adpSync.patchWorkerAdpStatus, {
 266:         employeeProfileId,
 267:         tenantId,
 268:         adpSyncStatus: 'synced',
 269:         adpAssociateOid: result.associateOID,
 270:         adpWorkerId: result.workerID,
 271:       })
 272: 
 273:       await patchEvent(ctx, claim.eventId, {
 274:         status: 'success',
 275:         response: {
 276:           adpAssociateOid: result.associateOID,
 277:           adpWorkerId: result.workerID,
 278:         },
 279:         completedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
 280:       })
 281: 
 282:       return { status: 'synced', adpAssociateOid: result.associateOID }
 283:     } catch (error) {
 284:       const isNotConfigured = error instanceof NotConfiguredError
 285:       const syncStatus = isNotConfigured ? 'pending_credentials' : 'error'
 286:       const adpError = sanitizeError(error)
 287:       const nextRetryAt =
 288:         !isNotConfigured && claim.attempt < MAX_ATTEMPTS
 289:           ? new Date(
 290:               Date.now() + chooseRetryDelayMs(claim.attempt),
 291:             ).toISOString()
 292:           : undefined
 293: 
 294:       await patchEvent(ctx, claim.eventId, {
 295:         status: syncStatus,
 296:         response: { error: adpError },
 297:         completedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
 298:         nextRetryAt,
 299:       })
 300: 
 301:       await ctx.runMutation(internal.adpSync.patchWorkerAdpStatus, {
 302:         employeeProfileId,
 303:         tenantId,
 304:         adpSyncStatus: syncStatus,
 305:         adpError,
 306:       })
 307: 
 308:       if (!isNotConfigured && claim.attempt < MAX_ATTEMPTS) {
 309:         await ctx.scheduler.runAfter(
 310:           chooseRetryDelayMs(claim.attempt),
 311:           internal.adpOutbound.adpSyncWorker,
 312:           { employeeProfileId },
 313:         )
 314:       }
 315: 
 316:       return { status: syncStatus, error: adpError }
 317:     }
 318:   },
 319: })

 321: export const adpInitialWorkerLoad = internalAction({
 322:   args: { tenantId: v.id('tenants') },
 323:   handler: async (ctx: ActionCtx, { tenantId }) => {
 324:     const configured = await ctx.runQuery(
 325:       internal.adpSync.isAdpConfiguredForTenant,
 326:       { tenantId },
 327:     )
 328:     if (!configured) {
 329:       return {
 330:         status: 'pending_credentials',
 331:         processed: 0,
 332:         matched: 0,
 333:         created: 0,
 334:         errors: 0,
 335:       }
 336:     }
 337: 
 338:     const key = idempotencyKey('initial_worker_load', tenantId as string)
 339:     const claim = await ctx.runMutation(
 340:       internal.adpSync.claimIntegrationEvent,
 341:       {
 342:         tenantId,
 343:         kind: 'initial_worker_load',
 344:         refId: tenantId as string,
 345:         idempotencyKey: key,
 346:         request: { tenantId: tenantId as string },
 347:         maxAttempts: MAX_ATTEMPTS,
 348:       },
 349:     )
 350: 
 351:     if (claim.type === 'already_synced') {
 352:       const response = claim.response as
 353:         | { processed: number; matched: number; created: number; errors: number }
 354:         | undefined
 355:       return {
 356:         status: 'already_synced',
 357:         processed: response?.processed ?? 0,
 358:         matched: response?.matched ?? 0,
 359:         created: response?.created ?? 0,
 360:         errors: response?.errors ?? 0,
 361:       }
 362:     }
 363: 
 364:     if (claim.type === 'in_flight') {
 365:       return {
 366:         status: 'in_flight',
 367:         processed: 0,
 368:         matched: 0,
 369:         created: 0,
 370:         errors: 0,
 371:       }
 372:     }
 373: 
 374:     if (claim.type === 'max_attempts') {
 375:       return {
 376:         status: 'max_attempts',
 377:         processed: 0,
 378:         matched: 0,
 379:         created: 0,
 380:         errors: 0,
 381:       }
 382:     }
 383: 
 384:     try {
 385:       const profiles = (await ctx.runQuery(
 386:         internal.adpSync.findEmployeeProfilesByTenant,
 387:         { tenantId },
 388:       )) as Doc<'employeeProfiles'>[]
 389: 
 390:       const profilesByEmail = new Map(
 391:         profiles.map((p: Doc<'employeeProfiles'>) => [
 392:           normalizeEmail(p.email),
 393:           p,
 394:         ]),
 395:       )
 396:       const profilesByName = new Map(
 397:         profiles.map((p: Doc<'employeeProfiles'>) => [
 398:           p.displayName.trim(),
 399:           p,
 400:         ]),
 401:       )
 402:       const profilesByAoid = new Map(
 403:         profiles
 404:           .filter(
 405:             (p: Doc<'employeeProfiles'>): p is Doc<'employeeProfiles'> & {
 406:               adpAssociateOid: string
 407:             } => Boolean(p.adpAssociateOid),
 408:           )
 409:           .map((p: Doc<'employeeProfiles'> & { adpAssociateOid: string }) => [
 410:             p.adpAssociateOid,
 411:             p,
 412:           ]),
 413:       )
 414: 
 415:       const port = await getAdpPort()
 416:       let cursor: string | undefined
 417:       let processed = 0
 418:       let matched = 0
 419:       let created = 0
 420:       let errors = 0
 421: 
 422:       do {
 423:         const page = await port.listWorkers(cursor)
 424:         for (const worker of page.workers) {
 425:           processed++
 426: 
 427:           if (!worker.associateOID || !worker.displayName.trim()) {
 428:             errors++
 429:             continue
 430:           }
 431: 
 432:           try {
 433:             const email = worker.email
 434:               ? normalizeEmail(worker.email)
 435:               : undefined
 436:             const matchByAoid = worker.associateOID
 437:               ? profilesByAoid.get(worker.associateOID)
 438:               : undefined
 439:             const matchByEmail = email
 440:               ? profilesByEmail.get(email)
 441:               : undefined
 442:             const matchByName = profilesByName.get(worker.displayName.trim())
 443:             const match = matchByAoid ?? matchByEmail ?? matchByName
 444: 
 445:             await ctx.runMutation(
 446:               internal.adpSync.upsertMatchedEmployeeProfile,
 447:               {
 448:                 tenantId,
 449:                 associateOID: worker.associateOID,
 450:                 workerID: worker.workerID,
 451:                 displayName: worker.displayName,
 452:                 email: email ?? worker.displayName,
 453:                 matchProfileId: match?._id,
 454:               },
 455:             )
 456: 
 457:             if (match) matched++
 458:             else created++
 459:           } catch (workerError) {
 460:             errors++
 461:             console.error('ADP worker load failed for a record:', workerError)
 462:           }
 463:         }
 464:         cursor = page.nextCursor
 465:       } while (cursor)
 466: 
 467:       const result = { processed, matched, created, errors }
 468:       await patchEvent(ctx, claim.eventId, {
 469:         status: 'success',
 470:         response: result,
 471:         completedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
 472:       })
 473: 
 474:       return { status: 'success', ...result }
 475:     } catch (error) {
 476:       const isNotConfigured = error instanceof NotConfiguredError
 477:       const syncStatus = isNotConfigured ? 'pending_credentials' : 'error'
 478:       const adpError = sanitizeError(error)
 479:       const nextRetryAt =
 480:         !isNotConfigured && claim.attempt < MAX_ATTEMPTS
 481:           ? new Date(
 482:               Date.now() + chooseRetryDelayMs(claim.attempt),
 483:             ).toISOString()
 484:           : undefined
 485: 
 486:       await patchEvent(ctx, claim.eventId, {
 487:         status: syncStatus,
 488:         response: { error: adpError },
 489:         completedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
 490:         nextRetryAt,
 491:       })
 492: 
 493:       if (!isNotConfigured && claim.attempt < MAX_ATTEMPTS) {
 494:         await ctx.scheduler.runAfter(
 495:           chooseRetryDelayMs(claim.attempt),
 496:           internal.adpOutbound.adpInitialWorkerLoad,
 497:           { tenantId },
 498:         )
 499:       }
 500: 
 501:       return { status: syncStatus, error: adpError, errors: 0 }
 502:     }
 503:   },
 504: })

 132:

  31: export class MockAdp implements AdpPort {
  32:   private workers: Map<string, AdpWorkerSummary>
  33:   private workersByIdempotencyKey: Map<string, AdpWorkerResult>
  34:   private punches: Map<string, AdpPunchResult>
  35:   private failNextCall: boolean
  36:   private failureMessage: string
  37: 
  38:   callCounts = {
  39:     getAccessToken: 0,
  40:     listWorkers: 0,
  41:     createWorker: 0,
  42:     postPunch: 0,
  43:     getTimeCards: 0,
  44:   }
  45: 
  46:   constructor(options: MockAdpOptions = {}) {
  47:     this.workers = new Map()
  48:     this.workersByIdempotencyKey = new Map()
  49:     this.punches = new Map()
  50:     this.failNextCall = options.failNextCall ?? false
  51:     this.failureMessage = options.failureMessage ?? 'Mock ADP failure'
  52: 
  53:     for (const worker of options.workers ?? []) {
  54:       this.workers.set(worker.associateOID, worker)
  55:     }
  56:   }
  57: 
  58:   private assertAvailable() {
  59:     if (this.failNextCall) {
  60:       this.failNextCall = false
  61:       throw new Error(this.failureMessage)
  62:     }
  63:   }
  64: 
  65:   async getAccessToken(): Promise<AdpAccessToken> {
  66:     this.callCounts.getAccessToken++
  67:     return {
  68:       accessToken: 'mock-access-token',
  69:       tokenType: 'Bearer',
  70:       expiresAt: new Date(Date.now() + 3_600_000).toISOString(),
  71:     }
  72:   }
  73: 
  74:   async listWorkers(cursor?: string): Promise<AdpListWorkersResult> {
  75:     this.assertAvailable()
  76:     this.callCounts.listWorkers++
  77:     const all = Array.from(this.workers.values())
  78:     const skip = cursor ? Number.parseInt(cursor, 10) : 0
  79:     const page = all.slice(skip, skip + 100)
  80:     const nextCursor = skip + page.length < all.length
  81:       ? String(skip + page.length)
  82:       : undefined
  83:     return { workers: page, nextCursor }
  84:   }
  85: 
  86:   async createWorker(
  87:     profile: AdpWorkerProfileInput,
  88:   ): Promise<AdpWorkerResult> {
  89:     this.assertAvailable()
  90:     this.callCounts.createWorker++
  91: 
  92:     const cached = this.workersByIdempotencyKey.get(profile.idempotencyKey)
  93:     if (cached) return cached
  94: 
  95:     const associateOID = stableAoid(profile.email)
  96:     const workerID = stableWorkerId(profile.email)
  97:     const worker: AdpWorkerSummary = {
  98:       associateOID,
  99:       workerID,
 100:       displayName: profile.displayName,
 101:       email: profile.email,
 102:       status: 'active',
 103:     }
 104:     const result = { associateOID, workerID }
 105:     this.workers.set(associateOID, worker)
 106:     this.workersByIdempotencyKey.set(profile.idempotencyKey, result)
 107:     return result
 108:   }
 109: 
 110:   async postPunch(punch: AdpPunchInput): Promise<AdpPunchResult> {
 111:     this.assertAvailable()
 112:     this.callCounts.postPunch++
 113:     const punchId = `mock-punch-${punch.idempotencyKey}`
 114:     const result: AdpPunchResult = { punchId }
 115:     this.punches.set(punch.idempotencyKey, result)
 116:     return result
 117:   }
 118: 
 119:   async getTimeCards(aoid: string): Promise<AdpTimeCard> {
 120:     this.assertAvailable()
 121:     this.callCounts.getTimeCards++
 122:     return { aoid, timeCards: [] }
 123:   }
 124: 
 125:   getPunch(idempotencyKey: string): AdpPunchResult | undefined {
 126:     return this.punches.get(idempotencyKey)
 127:   }
 128: 
 129:   getWorker(associateOID: string): AdpWorkerSummary | undefined {
 130:     return this.workers.get(associateOID)
 131:   }
 132: 
 133:   workerCount(): number {
 134:     return this.workers.size
 135:   }
 136: 
 137:   injectFailure(message?: string) {
 138:     this.failNextCall = true
 139:     if (message) this.failureMessage = message
 140:   }
 141: 
 142:   clearFailure() {
 143:     this.failNextCall = false
 144:     this.failureMessage = 'Mock ADP failure'
 145:   }
 146: }
 147: 
 148: let sharedMock: MockAdp | null = null
 149: 
 150: export function getSharedMockAdp(options?: MockAdpOptions): MockAdp {
 151:   if (!sharedMock) {
 152:     sharedMock = new MockAdp(options)
 153:   }
 154:   return sharedMock
 155: }

 164: export class AdpClient implements AdpPort {
 165:   async getAccessToken(): Promise<AdpAccessToken> {
 166:     const now = Date.now()
 167:     if (tokenCache && tokenCache.expiresAt > now + 60_000) {
 168:       return {
 169:         accessToken: tokenCache.accessToken,
 170:         tokenType: 'Bearer',
 171:         expiresAt: new Date(tokenCache.expiresAt).toISOString(),
 172:       }
 173:     }
 174: 
 175:     const tokenUrl = requireEnv('ADP_TOKEN_URL')
 176:     const clientId = requireEnv('ADP_CLIENT_ID')
 177:     const clientSecret = requireEnv('ADP_CLIENT_SECRET')
 178: 
 179:     const credentials = Buffer.from(`${clientId}:${clientSecret}`).toString(
 180:       'base64',
 181:     )
 182:     const { status, body } = await httpsRequest({
 183:       url: tokenUrl,
 184:       method: 'POST',
 185:       headers: {
 186:         Authorization: `Basic ${credentials}`,
 187:         'Content-Type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded',
 188:       },
 189:       body: 'grant_type=client_credentials',
 190:     })
 191: 
 192:     if (status < 200 || status >= 300) {
 193:       throw new Error(`ADP token request failed with status ${status}`)
 194:     }
 195: 
 196:     const payload = parseJson(body) as Record<string, unknown> | null
 197:     if (!payload || typeof payload.access_token !== 'string') {
 198:       throw new Error('ADP token response missing access_token')
 199:     }
 200: 
 201:     const expiresIn =
 202:       typeof payload.expires_in === 'number'
 203:         ? payload.expires_in
 204:         : Number.parseInt(String(payload.expires_in ?? '3600'), 10)
 205: 
 206:     const accessToken = payload.access_token
 207:     const expiresAt = now + expiresIn * 1000
 208:     tokenCache = { accessToken, expiresAt }
 209: 
 210:     return {
 211:       accessToken,
 212:       tokenType: String(payload.token_type ?? 'Bearer'),
 213:       expiresAt: new Date(expiresAt).toISOString(),
 214:     }
 215:   }
 216: 
 217:   private async adpGet(path: string): Promise<unknown> {
 218:     const baseUrl = requireEnv('ADP_BASE_URL')
 219:     const token = await this.getAccessToken()
 220:     const { status, body } = await httpsRequest({
 221:       url: `${baseUrl}${path}`,
 222:       method: 'GET',
 223:       headers: { Authorization: `${token.tokenType} ${token.accessToken}` },
 224:     })
 225: 
 226:     if (status < 200 || status >= 300) {
 227:       throw new Error(`ADP request failed: GET ${path} status ${status}`)
 228:     }
 229: 
 230:     return parseJson(body)
 231:   }
 232: 
 233:   private async adpPost(
 234:     path: string,
 235:     payload: Record<string, unknown>,
 236:   ): Promise<unknown> {
 237:     const baseUrl = requireEnv('ADP_BASE_URL')
 238:     const token = await this.getAccessToken()
 239:     const { status, body } = await httpsRequest({
 240:       url: `${baseUrl}${path}`,
 241:       method: 'POST',
 242:       headers: {
 243:         Authorization: `${token.tokenType} ${token.accessToken}`,
 244:         'Content-Type': 'application/json',
 245:       },
 246:       body: JSON.stringify(payload),
 247:     })
 248: 
 249:     if (status < 200 || status >= 300) {
 250:       throw new Error(`ADP request failed: POST ${path} status ${status}`)
 251:     }
 252: 
 253:     return parseJson(body)
 254:   }
 255: 
 256:   async listWorkers(cursor?: string): Promise<AdpListWorkersResult> {
 257:     const skip = cursor ? Number.parseInt(cursor, 10) : 0
 258:     const payload = await this.adpGet(`/hr/v2/workers?$top=100&$skip=${skip}`)
 259:     const workers: AdpWorkerSummary[] = []
 260: 
 261:     if (payload && typeof payload === 'object') {
 262:       const rawWorkers = (payload as Record<string, unknown>).workers
 263:       if (Array.isArray(rawWorkers)) {
 264:         for (const raw of rawWorkers) {
 265:           if (raw && typeof raw === 'object') {
 266:             workers.push(normalizeWorker(raw as Record<string, unknown>))
 267:           }
 268:         }
 269:       }
 270:     }
 271: 
 272:     const nextCursor = workers.length === 100 ? String(skip + 100) : undefined
 273:     return { workers, nextCursor }
 274:   }
 275: 
 276:   async createWorker(
 277:     profile: AdpWorkerProfileInput,
 278:   ): Promise<AdpWorkerResult> {
 279:     const payload = await this.adpPost('/events/hr/v1/worker.hire', {
 280:       events: [
 281:         {
 282:           data: {
 283:             eventContext: {
 284:               correlationID: profile.idempotencyKey,
 285:               associateOID: profile.employeeProfileId,
 286:             },
 287:             transform: {
 288:               worker: {
 289:                 person: {
 290:                   communication: {
 291:                     emails: [{ emailUri: profile.email }],
 292:                   },
 293:                   legalName: {
 294:                     formattedName: profile.displayName,
 295:                   },
 296:                 },
 297:               },
 298:             },
 299:           },
 300:         },
 301:       ],
 302:     })
 303: 
 304:     const eventPayload = payload && typeof payload === 'object'
 305:       ? (payload as Record<string, unknown>)
 306:       : {}
 307:     const events = eventPayload.events
 308:     const firstEvent = Array.isArray(events) && events.length > 0
 309:       ? events[0]
 310:       : null
 311:     const data = firstEvent && typeof firstEvent === 'object'
 312:       ? (firstEvent as Record<string, unknown>).data
 313:       : null
 314:     const output = data && typeof data === 'object'
 315:       ? (data as Record<string, unknown>).output
 316:       : null
 317:     const associateOID = output && typeof output === 'object'
 318:       ? String((output as Record<string, unknown>).associateOID ?? '')
 319:       : ''
 320:     const workerID = output && typeof output === 'object'
 321:       ? String((output as Record<string, unknown>).workerID ?? '')
 322:       : undefined
 323: 
 324:     if (!associateOID) {
 325:       throw new Error('ADP worker.hire response missing associateOID')
 326:     }
 327: 
 328:     return { associateOID, workerID }
 329:   }
 330: 
 331:   // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-unused-vars

 339:     )
 340:   }

  26: 
  27: type RoleValue = (typeof roleOptions)[number]['value']
  28: 
  29: /** Map ATRIA-X roles to Clerk's default organization roles.
  30:  *  Clerk only knows `org:admin` and `org:member` out of the box.
  31:  *  The real ATRIA-X role is stored in Convex `tenantMembers`.
  32:  */
  33: function toClerkRole(atriaRole: RoleValue): 'org:admin' | 'org:member' {

  35: }

  37: type ClerkInvitation = {
  38:   id: string
  39:   emailAddress: string
  40:   role: string

  42:   status: 'pending' | 'accepted' | 'revoked' | 'expired'
  43:   createdAt: Date
  44:   revoke: () => Promise<unknown>
  45: }
  46: 
  47: type CachedInvitation = {
  48:   id: string
  49:   emailAddress: string
  50:   role: string
  51:   status: string
  52:   createdAt: string
  53: }
  54: 
  55: function adpStatusPill(status: string) {
  56:   switch (status) {
  57:     case 'synced':
  58:     case 'matched':
  59:     case 'created':
  60:       return { label: 'Synced', variant: 'success' as const }
  61:     case 'queued':
  62:       return { label: 'Queued', variant: 'warning' as const }
  63:     case 'pending_credentials':
  64:       return { label: 'Pending credentials', variant: 'default' as const }
  65:     case 'error':
  66:       return { label: 'Error', variant: 'danger' as const }
  67:     default:
  68:       return { label: status, variant: 'default' as const }
  69:   }
  70: }
  71: 
  72: function getCacheKey(orgId: string) {
  73:   return `atria_pending_invites_${orgId}`
  74: }
  75: 
  76: function readCachedInvitations(orgId: string): CachedInvitation[] {
  77:   if (typeof window === 'undefined') return []
  78:   try {
  79:     const raw = localStorage.getItem(getCacheKey(orgId))
  80:     return raw ? (JSON.parse(raw) as CachedInvitation[]) : []
  81:   } catch {
  82:     return []
  83:   }
  84: }
  85: 
  86: function writeCachedInvitations(orgId: string, items: CachedInvitation[]) {
  87:   if (typeof window === 'undefined') return
  88:   try {
  89:     localStorage.setItem(getCacheKey(orgId), JSON.stringify(items))
  90:   } catch {
  91:     // ignore
  92:   }
  93: }
  94: 
  95: function addCachedInvitation(orgId: string, item: CachedInvitation) {
  96:   const existing = readCachedInvitations(orgId)
  97:   const updated = existing.filter((i) => i.id !== item.id)
  98:   updated.push(item)
  99:   writeCachedInvitations(orgId, updated)
 100: }
 101: 
 102: function removeCachedInvitation(orgId: string, invitationId: string) {
 103:   const existing = readCachedInvitations(orgId)
 104:   const updated = existing.filter((i) => i.id !== invitationId)
 105:   writeCachedInvitations(orgId, updated)
 106: }
 107: 
 108: function fromCache(cached: CachedInvitation): ClerkInvitation {
 109:   return {
 110:     ...cached,
 111:     createdAt: new Date(cached.createdAt),
 112:     roleName: cached.role,
 113:     status: cached.status as ClerkInvitation['status'],
 114:     revoke: () => Promise.resolve(),

 116: }
 117: 
 118: function cacheClerkInvitations(orgId: string, invitations: ClerkInvitation[]) {
 119:   writeCachedInvitations(
 120:     orgId,
 121:     invitations.map((invitation) => ({
 122:       id: invitation.id,
 123:       emailAddress: invitation.emailAddress,
 124:       role: invitation.role,
 125:       status: invitation.status,
 126:       createdAt:
 127:         invitation.createdAt instanceof Date
 128:           ? invitation.createdAt.toISOString()
 129:           : new Date().toISOString(),
 130:     })),
 131:   )
 132: }
 133: 
 134: export function TeamPage() {
 135:   const { organization, isLoaded: orgLoaded } = useOrganization()
 136:   const { user } = useUser()
 137:   const clerkOrgId = organization?.id
 138: 
 139:   const members = useQuery(
 140:     api.members.list,
 141:     clerkOrgId ? { clerkOrgId } : 'skip',
 142:   )
 143:   const employeeProfiles = useQuery(
 144:     api.employeeProfiles.listEmployeeProfiles,
 145:     clerkOrgId ? { clerkOrgId } : 'skip',
 146:   )
 147:   const updateRole = useMutation(api.members.updateRole)
 148:   const createInvitation = useAction(api.invitations.create)
 149:   const createCaregiver = useAction(api.employeeProfiles.createCaregiver)
 150:   const runAdpInitialWorkerLoad = useAction(
 151:     api.employeeProfiles.runAdpInitialWorkerLoad,
 152:   )
 153: 
 154:   const [inviteEmail, setInviteEmail] = useState('')
 155:   const [inviteRole, setInviteRole] = useState<RoleValue>('org:caregiver')
 156:   const [isInviting, setIsInviting] = useState(false)
 157:   const [inviteError, setInviteError] = useState<string | null>(null)
 158:   const [inviteSuccess, setInviteSuccess] = useState<string | null>(null)
 159:   const [roleUpdates, setRoleUpdates] = useState<Record<string, RoleValue>>({})
 160:   const [isUpdatingRole, setIsUpdatingRole] = useState<string | null>(null)
 161:   const [roleSyncError, setRoleSyncError] = useState<string | null>(null)
 162: 
 163:   const [caregiverName, setCaregiverName] = useState('')
 164:   const [caregiverEmail, setCaregiverEmail] = useState('')
 165:   const [isCreatingCaregiver, setIsCreatingCaregiver] = useState(false)
 166:   const [caregiverMessage, setCaregiverMessage] = useState<string | null>(null)
 167:   const [caregiverError, setCaregiverError] = useState<string | null>(null)
 168: 
 169:   const [isLoadingAdp, setIsLoadingAdp] = useState(false)
 170:   const [adpMessage, setAdpMessage] = useState<string | null>(null)
 171:   const [adpError, setAdpError] = useState<string | null>(null)
 172: 
 173:   const [invitations, setInvitations] = useState<ClerkInvitation[]>(() => {
 174:     if (!clerkOrgId) return []
 175:     const cached = readCachedInvitations(clerkOrgId)
 176:     return cached.map(fromCache)
 177:   })
 178:   const [isLoadingInvitations, setIsLoadingInvitations] = useState(false)
 179:   const [isRevoking, setIsRevoking] = useState<string | null>(null)
 180: 
 181:   const loadInvitations = useCallback(
 182:     async (attempt = 1): Promise<void> => {
 183:       if (!organization || !orgLoaded || !clerkOrgId) return
 184:       setIsLoadingInvitations(true)
 185:       try {
 186:         const result = (await organization.getInvitations({
 187:           status: ['pending'],
 188:           pageSize: 100,
 189:         })) as {
 190:           data: ClerkInvitation[]
 191:         }
 192:         const clerkData = result.data ?? []
 193:         cacheClerkInvitations(clerkOrgId, clerkData)
 194:         setInvitations(clerkData)
 195:         setInviteError(null)
 196:       } catch (err) {
 197:         const message =
 198:           err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to load invitations'
 199:         if (attempt < 2) {
 200:           await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 500))
 201:           return loadInvitations(attempt + 1)
 202:         }
 203:         // On total failure, keep cached invitations so they don't disappear
 204:         setInviteError(message)
 205:       } finally {
 206:         setIsLoadingInvitations(false)
 207:       }
 208:     },
 209:     [organization, orgLoaded, clerkOrgId],
 210:   )
 211: 
 212:   useEffect(() => {
 213:     if (!organization || !orgLoaded || !clerkOrgId) return
 214:     let cancelled = false
 215: 
 216:     const load = async () => {
 217:       try {
 218:         setIsLoadingInvitations(true)
 219:         const result = (await organization.getInvitations({
 220:           status: ['pending'],
 221:           pageSize: 100,
 222:         })) as {
 223:           data: ClerkInvitation[]
 224:         }
 225:         const clerkData = result.data ?? []
 226:         cacheClerkInvitations(clerkOrgId, clerkData)
 227:         if (!cancelled) {
 228:           setInvitations(clerkData)
 229:           setInviteError(null)
 230:         }
 231:       } catch (err) {
 232:         if (!cancelled) {
 233:           const message =
 234:             err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to load invitations'
 235:           setInviteError(message)
 236:         }
 237:       } finally {
 238:         if (!cancelled) setIsLoadingInvitations(false)
 239:       }
 240:     }
 241: 
 242:     load()
 243:     return () => {
 244:       cancelled = true
 245:     }
 246:   }, [organization, organization?.id, orgLoaded, clerkOrgId])
 247: 
 248:   const handleInvite = useCallback(async () => {
 249:     if (!inviteEmail.trim() || !clerkOrgId) return
 250:     setIsInviting(true)
 251:     setInviteError(null)
 252:     setInviteSuccess(null)
 253: 
 254:     try {
 255:       const created = await createInvitation({
 256:         clerkOrgId,
 257:         emailAddress: inviteEmail.trim(),
 258:         role: inviteRole,
 259:         appBaseUrl: window.location.origin,
 260:       })
 261:       const invitation = fromCache({
 262:         id: created.id,
 263:         emailAddress: created.emailAddress,
 264:         role: created.role,
 265:         status: created.status,
 266:         createdAt: created.createdAt,
 267:       })
 268: 
 269:       // Cache the invitation locally so it survives navigation
 270:       addCachedInvitation(clerkOrgId, {
 271:         id: invitation.id,
 272:         emailAddress: invitation.emailAddress,
 273:         role: invitation.role,
 274:         status: invitation.status,
 275:         createdAt:
 276:           invitation.createdAt instanceof Date
 277:             ? invitation.createdAt.toISOString()
 278:             : new Date().toISOString(),
 279:       })
 280: 
 281:       // Merge into current state immediately
 282:       setInvitations((prev) => {
 283:         const existing = prev.filter((i) => i.id !== invitation.id)
 284:         return [...existing, invitation]
 285:       })
 286: 
 287:       if (invitation.status === 'accepted') {
 288:         setInviteSuccess(`${inviteEmail.trim()} joined the organization.`)
 289:       } else if (invitation.status === 'pending') {
 290:         setInviteSuccess(`Invitation sent to ${inviteEmail.trim()}`)
 291:       } else {
 292:         setInviteSuccess(`Invitation status: ${invitation.status}`)
 293:       }
 294:       setInviteEmail('')
 295:       await loadInvitations()
 296:     } catch (err) {
 297:       setInviteError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Invite failed')
 298:     } finally {
 299:       setIsInviting(false)
 300:     }
 301:   }, [createInvitation, inviteEmail, inviteRole, clerkOrgId, loadInvitations])
 302: 
 303:   const handleRevoke = useCallback(
 304:     async (invitation: ClerkInvitation) => {
 305:       setIsRevoking(invitation.id)
 306:       try {
 307:         // Cached-only invitations have a no-op revoke; just remove from cache
 308:         const isCachedOnly =
 309:           typeof invitation.revoke !== 'function' ||
 310:           invitation.revoke.toString() === '() => Promise.resolve()'
 311:         if (isCachedOnly) {
 312:           if (clerkOrgId) removeCachedInvitation(clerkOrgId, invitation.id)
 313:           setInvitations((prev) => prev.filter((i) => i.id !== invitation.id))
 314:         } else {
 315:           await invitation.revoke()
 316:           if (clerkOrgId) removeCachedInvitation(clerkOrgId, invitation.id)
 317:           await loadInvitations()
 318:         }
 319:       } catch (err) {
 320:         setInviteError(
 321:           err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to revoke invitation',
 322:         )
 323:       } finally {
 324:         setIsRevoking(null)
 325:       }
 326:     },
 327:     [loadInvitations, clerkOrgId],
 328:   )
 329: 
 330:   const handleCreateCaregiver = useCallback(async () => {
 331:     if (!clerkOrgId || !caregiverName.trim() || !caregiverEmail.trim()) return
 332:     setIsCreatingCaregiver(true)
 333:     setCaregiverError(null)
 334:     setCaregiverMessage(null)
 335: 
 336:     try {
 337:       await createCaregiver({
 338:         clerkOrgId,
 339:         displayName: caregiverName.trim(),
 340:         email: caregiverEmail.trim(),
 341:         appBaseUrl: window.location.origin,
 342:       })
 343:       setCaregiverMessage(`Invitation sent to ${caregiverEmail.trim()}`)
 344:       setCaregiverName('')
 345:       setCaregiverEmail('')
 346:     } catch (err) {
 347:       setCaregiverError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to add caregiver')
 348:     } finally {
 349:       setIsCreatingCaregiver(false)
 350:     }
 351:   }, [clerkOrgId, caregiverName, caregiverEmail, createCaregiver])
 352: 
 353:   const handleLoadAdp = useCallback(async () => {
 354:     if (!clerkOrgId) return
 355:     setIsLoadingAdp(true)
 356:     setAdpError(null)
 357:     setAdpMessage(null)
 358: 
 359:     try {
 360:       const result = await runAdpInitialWorkerLoad({ clerkOrgId })
 361:       if (result.status === 'success') {
 362:         setAdpMessage(
 363:           `ADP load complete: ${result.processed} processed, ${result.matched} matched, ${result.created} created, ${result.errors} errors`,
 364:         )
 365:       } else {
 366:         setAdpMessage(
 367:           `ADP load ${result.status}${result.error ? `: ${result.error}` : ''}`,
 368:         )
 369:       }
 370:     } catch (err) {
 371:       setAdpError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'ADP load failed')
 372:     } finally {
 373:       setIsLoadingAdp(false)
 374:     }
 375:   }, [clerkOrgId, runAdpInitialWorkerLoad])
 376: 
 377:   async function syncConvexRole(
 378:     clerkUserId: string,
 379:     newRole: RoleValue,
 380:     attempt = 1,
 381:   ): Promise<void> {
 382:     if (!clerkOrgId) return
 383:     try {
 384:       await updateRole({ clerkOrgId, clerkUserId, role: newRole })
 385:       setRoleSyncError(null)
 386:     } catch (err) {
 387:       if (attempt < 2) {
 388:         await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 500))
 389:         return syncConvexRole(clerkUserId, newRole, attempt + 1)
 390:       }
 391:       throw err
 392:     }
 393:   }
 394: 
 395:   const handleRoleChange = useCallback(
 396:     async (clerkUserId: string, newRole: RoleValue) => {
 397:       if (!clerkOrgId || clerkUserId === user?.id) return
 398:       setIsUpdatingRole(clerkUserId)
 399:       setRoleSyncError(null)
 400:       setRoleUpdates((prev) => ({ ...prev, [clerkUserId]: newRole }))
 401: 
 402:       try {
 403:         await organization?.updateMember({
 404:           userId: clerkUserId,
 405:           role: toClerkRole(newRole),
 406:         })
 407:         await syncConvexRole(clerkUserId, newRole)
 408:       } catch (err) {
 409:         setRoleUpdates((prev) => {
 410:           const next = { ...prev }
 411:           delete next[clerkUserId]
 412:           return next
 413:         })
 414:         const message =
 415:           err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Role update failed'
 416:         setRoleSyncError(message)
 417:       } finally {
 418:         setIsUpdatingRole(null)
 419:       }
 420:     },
 421:     // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
 422:     [clerkOrgId, user?.id, organization],
 423:   )
 424: 
 425:   if (!members) {
 426:     return (
 427:       <div className="flex h-full items-center justify-center">
 428:         <div className="text-sm text-atria-muted">Loading teamâ€¦</div>
 429:       </div>
 430:     )
 431:   }
 432: 
 433:   const isAdmin =
 434:     members.find((m: Doc<'tenantMembers'>) => m.clerkUserId === user?.id)?.role ===
 435:     'org:admin'
 436: 
 437:   const memberEmails = new Set(
 438:     members
 439:       .map((member: Doc<'tenantMembers'>) => member.email.trim().toLowerCase())
 440:       .filter(Boolean),
 441:   )
 442:   const pendingInvitations = invitations.filter(
 443:     (i) =>
 444:       i.status === 'pending' &&
 445:       !memberEmails.has(i.emailAddress.trim().toLowerCase()),
 446:   )
 447: 
 448:   return (
 449:     <div className="space-y-6">
 450:       <div>
 451:         <h1 className="text-lg font-semibold text-atria-ink">Team</h1>
 452:         <p className="text-sm text-atria-muted">
 453:           {members.length} member{members.length !== 1 ? 's' : ''}
 454:         </p>
 455:       </div>
 456: 
 457:       {isAdmin && (
 458:         <Card>
 459:           <CardHeader>
 460:             <CardTitle className="flex items-center gap-2">
 461:               <UserPlus className="h-4 w-4 text-atria-accent" />
 462:               Invite Member
 463:             </CardTitle>
 464:           </CardHeader>
 465:           <CardContent className="space-y-3">
 466:             <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row">
 467:               <div className="flex-1">
 468:                 <label className="text-xs font-medium text-atria-muted uppercase tracking-wider">
 469:                   Email
 470:                 </label>
 471:                 <div className="relative mt-1">
 472:                   <Mail className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 h-4 w-4 -translate-y-1/2 text-atria-muted" />
 473:                   <Input
 474:                     type="email"
 475:                     placeholder="colleague@agency.com"
 476:                     value={inviteEmail}
 477:                     onChange={(e) => setInviteEmail(e.target.value)}
 478:                     className="pl-9"
 479:                   />
 480:                 </div>
 481:               </div>
 482:               <div className="sm:w-40">
 483:                 <label className="text-xs font-medium text-atria-muted uppercase tracking-wider">
 484:                   Role
 485:                 </label>
 486:                 <Select
 487:                   value={inviteRole}
 488:                   onChange={(e) => setInviteRole(e.target.value as RoleValue)}
 489:                   className="mt-1"
 490:                 >
 491:                   {roleOptions.map((opt) => (
 492:                     <option key={opt.value} value={opt.value}>
 493:                       {opt.label}
 494:                     </option>
 495:                   ))}
 496:                 </Select>
 497:               </div>
 498:               <div className="flex items-end">
 499:                 <Button
 500:                   variant="primary"
 501:                   onClick={handleInvite}
 502:                   disabled={isInviting || !inviteEmail.trim()}
 503:                 >
 504:                   {isInviting ? 'Sendingâ€¦' : 'Send invite'}
 505:                 </Button>
 506:               </div>
 507:             </div>
 508:             {inviteError && (
 509:               <p className="text-sm text-atria-danger">{inviteError}</p>
 510:             )}
 511:             {inviteSuccess && (
 512:               <p className="text-sm text-atria-success">{inviteSuccess}</p>
 513:             )}
 514:           </CardContent>
 515:         </Card>
 516:       )}
 517: 
 518:       {isAdmin && (
 519:         <Card>
 520:           <CardHeader>
 521:             <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
 522:               <CardTitle>Pending Invitations</CardTitle>
 523:               <Button
 524:                 variant="ghost"
 525:                 size="sm"
 526:                 onClick={() => loadInvitations()}
 527:                 disabled={isLoadingInvitations}
 528:                 className="text-atria-muted hover:text-atria-ink"
 529:               >
 530:                 <RefreshCw
 531:                   className={`h-4 w-4 ${isLoadingInvitations ? 'animate-spin' : ''}`}
 532:                 />
 533:                 Refresh
 534:               </Button>
 535:             </div>
 536:           </CardHeader>
 537:           <CardContent className="p-0">
 538:             {pendingInvitations.length === 0 ? (
 539:               <div className="px-4 py-6 text-sm text-atria-muted">
 540:                 {isLoadingInvitations
 541:                   ? 'Loading pending invitations...'
 542:                   : 'No pending invitations.'}
 543:               </div>
 544:             ) : (
 545:               <Table>
 546:                 <TableHead>
 547:                   <TableRow>
 548:                     <TableHeader>Email</TableHeader>
 549:                     <TableHeader>Status</TableHeader>
 550:                     <TableHeader className="w-24">Action</TableHeader>
 551:                   </TableRow>
 552:                 </TableHead>
 553:                 <TableBody>
 554:                   {pendingInvitations.map((invitation) => {
 555:                     const isCachedOnly =
 556:                       typeof invitation.revoke !== 'function' ||
 557:                       invitation.revoke.toString() === '() => Promise.resolve()'
 558:                     return (
 559:                       <TableRow key={invitation.id}>
 560:                         <TableCell>{invitation.emailAddress}</TableCell>
 561:                         <TableCell>
 562:                           <Badge variant="warning">Pending</Badge>
 563:                           {isCachedOnly && (
 564:                             <span className="ml-2 text-[10px] text-atria-muted">
 565:                               (cached)
 566:                             </span>
 567:                           )}
 568:                         </TableCell>
 569:                         <TableCell>
 570:                           <Button
 571:                             variant="ghost"
 572:                             size="sm"
 573:                             disabled={isRevoking === invitation.id}
 574:                             onClick={() => handleRevoke(invitation)}
 575:                             className="text-atria-danger hover:text-atria-danger/80"
 576:                           >
 577:                             <X className="h-4 w-4" />
 578:                             {isRevoking === invitation.id
 579:                               ? '...'
 580:                               : isCachedOnly
 581:                                 ? 'Remove'
 582:                                 : 'Revoke'}
 583:                           </Button>
 584:                         </TableCell>
 585:                       </TableRow>
 586:                     )
 587:                   })}
 588:                 </TableBody>
 589:               </Table>
 590:             )}
 591:           </CardContent>
 592:         </Card>
 593:       )}
 594: 
 595:       {isAdmin && employeeProfiles !== undefined && (
 596:         <Card>
 597:           <CardHeader>
 598:             <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
 599:               <CardTitle className="flex items-center gap-2">
 600:                 <UserPlus className="h-4 w-4 text-atria-accent" />
 601:                 Caregivers & ADP sync
 602:               </CardTitle>
 603:               <Button
 604:                 variant="secondary"
 605:                 size="sm"
 606:                 onClick={handleLoadAdp}
 607:                 disabled={isLoadingAdp}
 608:               >
 609:                 <RefreshCw
 610:                   className={`h-4 w-4 ${isLoadingAdp ? 'animate-spin' : ''}`}
 611:                 />
 612:                 Load from ADP
 613:               </Button>
 614:             </div>
 615:           </CardHeader>
 616:           <CardContent className="space-y-4">
 617:             <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
 618:               <div>
 619:                 <label className="text-xs font-medium text-atria-muted uppercase tracking-wider">
 620:                   Name
 621:                 </label>
 622:                 <Input
 623:                   value={caregiverName}
 624:                   onChange={(e) => setCaregiverName(e.target.value)}
 625:                   placeholder="Caregiver name"
 626:                   className="mt-1"
 627:                 />
 628:               </div>
 629:               <div>
 630:                 <label className="text-xs font-medium text-atria-muted uppercase tracking-wider">
 631:                   Email
 632:                 </label>
 633:                 <Input
 634:                   type="email"
 635:                   value={caregiverEmail}
 636:                   onChange={(e) => setCaregiverEmail(e.target.value)}
 637:                   placeholder="caregiver@agency.com"
 638:                   className="mt-1"
 639:                 />
 640:               </div>
 641:             </div>
 642:             <Button
 643:               variant="primary"
 644:               onClick={handleCreateCaregiver}
 645:               disabled={
 646:                 isCreatingCaregiver ||
 647:                 !caregiverName.trim() ||
 648:                 !caregiverEmail.trim()
 649:               }
 650:             >
 651:               {isCreatingCaregiver ? 'Addingâ€¦' : 'Add caregiver'}
 652:             </Button>
 653:             {caregiverError && (
 654:               <p className="text-sm text-atria-danger">{caregiverError}</p>
 655:             )}
 656:             {caregiverMessage && (
 657:               <p className="text-sm text-atria-success">{caregiverMessage}</p>
 658:             )}
 659:             {adpError && (
 660:               <p className="text-sm text-atria-danger">{adpError}</p>
 661:             )}
 662:             {adpMessage && (
 663:               <p className="text-sm text-atria-success">{adpMessage}</p>
 664:             )}
 665: 
 666:             {employeeProfiles.length === 0 ? (
 667:               <div className="text-sm text-atria-muted">
 668:                 No caregiver employee profiles yet.
 669:               </div>
 670:             ) : (
 671:               <Table>
 672:                 <TableHead>
 673:                   <TableRow>
 674:                     <TableHeader>Name</TableHeader>
 675:                     <TableHeader>Email</TableHeader>
 676:                     <TableHeader>Role</TableHeader>
 677:                     <TableHeader>ADP Status</TableHeader>
 678:                   </TableRow>
 679:                 </TableHead>
 680:                 <TableBody>
 681:                   {employeeProfiles.map((profile) => {
 682:                     const pill = adpStatusPill(profile.adpSyncStatus)
 683:                     return (
 684:                       <TableRow key={profile._id}>
 685:                         <TableCell className="font-medium">
 686:                           {profile.displayName}
 687:                         </TableCell>
 688:                         <TableCell>{profile.email}</TableCell>
 689:                         <TableCell>
 690:                           <Badge variant="success">
 691:                             {profile.role.replace('org:', '')}
 692:                           </Badge>
 693:                         </TableCell>
 694:                         <TableCell>
 695:                           <Badge variant={pill.variant}>{pill.label}</Badge>
 696:                         </TableCell>
 697:                       </TableRow>
 698:                     )
 699:                   })}
 700:                 </TableBody>
 701:               </Table>
 702:             )}
 703:           </CardContent>
 704:         </Card>
 705:       )}
 706: 
 707:       {roleSyncError && (
 708:         <div className="rounded-md bg-atria-danger-bg border border-atria-danger/20 px-4 py-3">
 709:           <p className="text-sm text-atria-danger font-medium">
 710:             Role sync failed: {roleSyncError}
 711:           </p>
 712:           <p className="text-xs text-atria-danger mt-1">
 713:             The Clerk role was updated, but the local database could not be
 714:             synced. Please refresh the page and try again.
 715:           </p>
 716:         </div>
 717:       )}
 718: 
 719:       <Card>
 720:         <CardHeader>
 721:           <CardTitle>Agency Members</CardTitle>
 722:         </CardHeader>
 723:         <CardContent className="p-0">
 724:           {members.length === 0 ? (
 725:             <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-12 text-center">
 726:               <div className="inline-flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-atria-border/50 mb-3">
 727:                 <Building2 className="h-6 w-6 text-atria-muted" />
 728:               </div>
 729:               <p className="text-sm font-medium text-atria-ink">
 730:                 No members yet
 731:               </p>
 732:               <p className="text-xs text-atria-muted mt-1 max-w-xs">
 733:                 Team members will appear here after they join the agency.
 734:               </p>
 735:             </div>
 736:           ) : (
 737:             <Table>
 738:               <TableHead>
 739:                 <TableRow>
 740:                   <TableHeader>Name</TableHeader>
 741:                   <TableHeader>Email</TableHeader>
 742:                   <TableHeader>Role</TableHeader>
 743:                 </TableRow>
 744:               </TableHead>
 745:               <TableBody>
 746:                 {members.map((member: Doc<'tenantMembers'>) => {
 747:                   const isSelf = member.clerkUserId === user?.id
 748:                   const currentRole =
 749:                     roleUpdates[member.clerkUserId] ?? member.role
 750: 
 751:                   return (
 752:                     <TableRow key={member._id}>
 753:                       <TableCell className="font-medium">
 754:                         {member.displayName}
 755:                         {isSelf && (

 757:                             (you)
 758:                           </span>

 760:                       </TableCell>

 762:                       <TableCell>

 774:                               className="w-32"
 775:                             >
 776:                               {roleOptions.map((opt) => (
 777:                                 <option key={opt.value} value={opt.value}>
 778:                                   {opt.label}
 779:                                 </option>

 781:                             </Select>
 782:                             {isUpdatingRole === member.clerkUserId && (

 785:                               </span>
 786:                             )}
 787:                           </div>
 788:                         ) : (
 789:                           <Badge
 790:                             variant={
 791:                               member.role === 'org:admin'

 793:                                 : member.role === 'org:coordinator'
 794:                                   ? 'info'
 795:                                   : 'success'
 796:                             }
 797:                           >
 798:                             {member.role.replace('org:', '')}
 799:                           </Badge>
 800:                         )}
 801:                       </TableCell>
 802:                     </TableRow>
 803:                   )
 804:                 })}
 805:               </TableBody>
 806:             </Table>
 807:           )}
 808:         </CardContent>
 809:       </Card>
 810:     </div>

  76: export const update = mutation({

 113: export const updateServiceAddress = mutation({
 114:   args: {
 115:     clerkOrgId: v.string(),
 116:     clientId: v.id('clients'),
 117:     serviceAddress: serviceAddressValidator,

 128:

 130:       if (

 157:         }
 158:       }
 159:     }
 160:

 162:       serviceAddress: args.serviceAddress,
 163:     })
 164:

 166:   },
 167: })

 169: export const remove = mutation({
 170:   args: { clerkOrgId: v.string(), clientId: v.id('clients') },
 171:   handler: async (ctx, args) => {
 172:     const { tenantId } = await requireTenantRole(ctx, args.clerkOrgId, [
 173:       'org:admin',
 174:     ])
 175: 
 176:     const client = await ctx.db.get(args.clientId)
 177:     if (!client) throw new Error('Client not found.')
 178:     assertTenantDoc(client, tenantId)
 179: 
 180:     const linkedShift = await ctx.db
 181:       .query('shifts')
 182:       .withIndex('by_tenant_status_start', (q) => q.eq('tenantId', tenantId))
 183:       .filter((q) => q.eq(q.field('clientId'), args.clientId))
 184:       .first()
 185: 
 186:     if (linkedShift) {
 187:       throw new Error(
 188:         'Cannot delete a client with scheduled or documented shifts.',
 189:       )
 190:     }
 191: 
 192:     await ctx.db.delete(args.clientId)
 193:     return args.clientId
 194:   },
 195: })

 136: async function getShiftGeofence(
 137:   ctx: MutationCtx,
 138:   tenantId: Id<'tenants'>,
 139: ) {
 140:   const settings = await ctx.db
 141:     .query('tenantSettings')
 142:     .withIndex('by_tenant', (q) => q.eq('tenantId', tenantId))
 143:     .unique()
 144: 
 145:   return settings?.shiftGeofence ?? DEFAULT_SHIFT_GEOFENCE
 146: }

 148: async function validateClockPunchLocation(
 149:   ctx: MutationCtx,
 150:   tenantId: Id<'tenants'>,
 151:   shift: {
 152:     clientId: Id<'clients'>
 153:     serviceLocationOverride?: {
 154:       label: string
 155:       latitude: number
 156:       longitude: number
 157:       radiusMeters?: number
 158:     } | null
 159:   },
 160:   location: LocationInput | undefined,
 161:   punchType: 'clock_in' | 'clock_out',
 162: ) {
 163:   const geofence = await getShiftGeofence(ctx, tenantId)
 164:   const required =
 165:     geofence.enabled &&
 166:     (punchType === 'clock_in'
 167:       ? geofence.enforceClockIn
 168:       : geofence.enforceClockOut)
 169: 
 170:   const client = await ctx.db.get(shift.clientId)
 171:   if (!client) throw new Error('Client not found.')
 172:   assertTenantDoc(client, tenantId)
 173: 
 174:   const target = resolveShiftServiceTarget(shift, client, geofence)
 175: 
 176:   return validatePunchLocation({
 177:     location,
 178:     target,
 179:     required,
 180:     maxAccuracyMeters: geofence.maxAccuracyMeters,
 181:   })
 182: }
