  15:   withinGeofence?: boolean
  16: }
  17: 
  18: export interface ServiceTarget extends LatLng {
  19:   label: string
  20:   radiusMeters: number
  21: }
  22: 
  23: const EARTH_RADIUS_METERS = 6_371_000
  24: 
  25: export function haversineDistanceMeters(from: LatLng, to: LatLng): number {
  26:   const toRadians = (degrees: number) => (degrees * Math.PI) / 180
  27:   const deltaLatitude = toRadians(to.latitude - from.latitude)
  28:   const deltaLongitude = toRadians(to.longitude - from.longitude)
  29: 
  30:   const a =
  31:     Math.sin(deltaLatitude / 2) ** 2 +
  32:     Math.cos(toRadians(from.latitude)) *
  33:       Math.cos(toRadians(to.latitude)) *
  34:       Math.sin(deltaLongitude / 2) ** 2
  35: 
  36:   const c = 2 * Math.atan2(Math.sqrt(a), Math.sqrt(1 - a))
  37:   return EARTH_RADIUS_METERS * c
  38: }
  39: 
  40: export function resolveShiftServiceTarget(
  41:   shift: {
  42:     serviceLocationOverride?: {
  43:       label: string
  44:       latitude: number
  45:       longitude: number
  46:       radiusMeters?: number
  47:     } | null
  48:   },
  49:   client: {
  50:     serviceAddress?: {
  51:       latitude?: number
  52:       longitude?: number
  53:       line1?: string
  54:     } | null
  55:   },
  56:   settings: { defaultRadiusMeters: number },
  57: ): ServiceTarget | null {
  58:   if (shift.serviceLocationOverride) {
  59:     return {
  60:       latitude: shift.serviceLocationOverride.latitude,
  61:       longitude: shift.serviceLocationOverride.longitude,
  62:       label: shift.serviceLocationOverride.label,
  63:       radiusMeters:
  64:         shift.serviceLocationOverride.radiusMeters ??
  65:         settings.defaultRadiusMeters,
  66:     }
  67:   }
  68: 
  69:   if (
  70:     client.serviceAddress?.latitude !== undefined &&
  71:     client.serviceAddress?.longitude !== undefined
  72:   ) {
  73:     return {
  74:       latitude: client.serviceAddress.latitude,
  75:       longitude: client.serviceAddress.longitude,
  76:       label: client.serviceAddress.line1 ?? 'Client service address',
  77:       radiusMeters: settings.defaultRadiusMeters,
  78:     }
  79:   }
  80: 
  81:   return null
  82: }
  83: 
  84: function assertValidCoordinate(latitude: number, longitude: number) {
  85:   if (!Number.isFinite(latitude) || latitude < -90 || latitude > 90) {
  86:     throw new Error('Invalid location: latitude must be between -90 and 90.')
  87:   }
  88: 
  89:   if (!Number.isFinite(longitude) || longitude < -180 || longitude > 180) {
  90:     throw new Error('Invalid location: longitude must be between -180 and 180.')
  91:   }
  92: }
  93: 
  94: export function validatePunchLocation({
  95:   location,
  96:   target,
  97:   required,
  98:   maxAccuracyMeters,
  99: }: {
 100:   location?: LocationInput | null
 101:   target: ServiceTarget | null
 102:   required: boolean
 103:   maxAccuracyMeters: number
 104: }): LocationEvidence | undefined {
 105:   if (!location) {
 106:     if (required) {
 107:       throw new Error(
 108:         'Location is required for this punch because geofence enforcement is enabled.',
 109:       )
 110:     }
 111: 
 112:     return undefined
 113:   }
 114: 
 115:   assertValidCoordinate(location.latitude, location.longitude)
 116: 
 117:   if (!Number.isFinite(location.accuracyMeters) || location.accuracyMeters <= 0) {
 118:     throw new Error('Invalid location: accuracyMeters must be a positive number.')
 119:   }
 120: 
 121:   if (required && location.accuracyMeters > maxAccuracyMeters) {
 122:     throw new Error(
 123:       `Location accuracy (${location.accuracyMeters.toFixed(1)}m) exceeds the agency limit of ${maxAccuracyMeters}m. Please move to an area with better GPS signal and try again.`,
 124:     )
 125:   }
 126: 
 127:   if (!target) {
 128:     if (required) {
 129:       throw new Error(
 130:         'No service location coordinates are configured for this shift. Contact your coordinator before clocking in/out.',
 131:       )
 132:     }
 133: 
 134:     return {
 135:       ...location,
 136:       withinGeofence: undefined,
 137:       targetLabel: undefined,
 138:       targetLatitude: undefined,
 139:       targetLongitude: undefined,
 140:       distanceMeters: undefined,
 141:     }
 142:   }
 143: 
 144:   if (!Number.isFinite(target.radiusMeters) || target.radiusMeters <= 0) {
 145:     throw new Error('Invalid geofence radius: radiusMeters must be positive.')
 146:   }
 147: 
 148:   const distanceMeters = haversineDistanceMeters(location, target)
 149:   const withinGeofence = distanceMeters <= target.radiusMeters
 150: 
 151:   if (required && !withinGeofence) {
 152:     throw new Error(
 153:       `You are ${Math.round(distanceMeters)}m from the service location (${target.label}) and outside the allowed ${Math.round(target.radiusMeters)}m radius. Move closer and try again.`,
 154:     )
 155:   }
 156: 
 157:   return {
 158:     ...location,
 159:     distanceMeters,
 160:     targetLabel: target.label,
 161:     targetLatitude: target.latitude,
 162:     targetLongitude: target.longitude,
 163:     withinGeofence,
 164:   }
 165: }

codex
The geofence mutation uses `requireTenantRole` for admin/coordinator and rejects caregivers on the server, which matches the core authorization requirement. I’m checking the ADP worker and employee-profile lifecycle more closely now because that’s where async state and idempotency bugs tend to hide.

  34: async function seedAgency(
  35:   t: ReturnType<typeof createTestConvex>,
  36:   options: {
  37:     clerkOrgId: string
  38:     caregiverId: string
  39:     caregiverEmail?: string
  40:     configureAdp?: boolean
  41:   },
  42: ) {
  43:   return t.run(async (ctx) => {
  44:     const tenantId = await ctx.db.insert('tenants', {
  45:       clerkOrgId: options.clerkOrgId,
  46:       name: 'Test Agency',
  47:       slug: 'test-agency',
  48:       createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
  49:     })
  50: 
  51:     const memberId = await ctx.db.insert('tenantMembers', {
  52:       tenantId,
  53:       clerkUserId: options.caregiverId,
  54:       role: 'org:caregiver',
  55:       displayName: 'Caregiver',
  56:       email: options.caregiverEmail ?? 'caregiver@example.com',
  57:     })
  58: 
  59:     if (options.configureAdp) {
  60:       await ctx.db.insert('integrationConnections', {
  61:         tenantId,
  62:         provider: 'adp',
  63:         status: 'configured',
  64:         lastCheckedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
  65:       })
  66:     }
  67: 
  68:     const clientId = await ctx.db.insert('clients', {
  69:       tenantId,
  70:       displayName: 'Test Client',
  71:       serviceType: 'SLS',
  72:       authorizationHours: 100,
  73:       riskFlags: [],
  74:       serviceAddress: {
  75:         line1: '123 Main St',
  76:         city: 'Minneapolis',
  77:         state: 'MN',
  78:         postalCode: '55401',
  79:       },
  80:     })
  81: 
  82:     const shiftId = await ctx.db.insert('shifts', {
  83:       tenantId,
  84:       clientId,
  85:       caregiverId: options.caregiverId,
  86:       scheduledStart: '2020-01-01T08:00:00Z',
  87:       scheduledEnd: '2020-01-01T16:00:00Z',
  88:       status: 'scheduled',
  89:       serviceType: 'SLS',
  90:       rate: 25,
  91:     })
  92: 
  93:     await ctx.db.insert('progressNotes', {
  94:       tenantId,
  95:       shiftId,
  96:       startTime: '',
  97:       endTime: '',
  98:       servicesProvided: '',
  99:       clientResponse: '',
 100:       narrative: '',
 101:     })
 102: 
 103:     await ctx.db.insert('shiftTasks', {
 104:       tenantId,
 105:       shiftId,
 106:       title: 'Upload shift documentation proof',
 107:       requiredProof: true,
 108:       status: 'pending',
 109:     })
 110: 
 111:     return { tenantId, memberId, clientId, shiftId }
 112:   })
 113: }

 115: function asCaregiver(
 116:   t: ReturnType<typeof createTestConvex>,
 117:   caregiverId: string,
 118:   clerkOrgId: string,
 119: ) {
 120:   return t.withIdentity({
 121:     subject: caregiverId,
 122:     org_id: clerkOrgId,
 123:     org_role: 'org:caregiver',
 124:   })
 125: }
 126: 
 127: function asAdmin(
 128:   t: ReturnType<typeof createTestConvex>,
 129:   adminId: string,
 130:   clerkOrgId: string,
 131: ) {
 132:   return t.withIdentity({
 133:     subject: adminId,
 134:     org_id: clerkOrgId,
 135:     org_role: 'org:admin',
 136:   })
 137: }

 366: describe('sanitizeError', () => {
 367:   it('redacts ADP env values, Basic/Bearer tokens, and generic tokens', () => {
 368:     vi.stubEnv('ADP_CLIENT_ID', 'mock-client-id')
 369:     vi.stubEnv('ADP_CLIENT_SECRET', 'mock-client-secret')
 370: 
 371:     const message =
 372:       'ADP request failed: client_id mock-client-id Basic bW9jay1jbGllbnQtaWQ6bW9jay1jbGllbnQtc2VjcmV0 token=mock-client-secret Bearer eyJhbGciOiJIUzI1NiIsInR5cCI6IkpXVCJ9'
 373: 
 374:     const redacted = sanitizeError(new Error(message))
 375: 
 376:     expect(redacted).not.toContain('mock-client-id')
 377:     expect(redacted).not.toContain('mock-client-secret')
 378:     expect(redacted).not.toContain(
 379:       'bW9jay1jbGllbnQtaWQ6bW9jay1jbGllbnQtc2VjcmV0',
 380:     )
 381:     expect(redacted).not.toContain('eyJhbGciOiJIUzI1NiIsInR5cCI6IkpXVCJ9')
 382:     expect(redacted).toContain('[REDACTED:ADP_CLIENT_ID]')
 383:     expect(redacted).toContain('[REDACTED:ADP_CLIENT_SECRET]')
 384:     expect(redacted).toContain('Basic [REDACTED]')
 385:     expect(redacted).toContain('Bearer [REDACTED]')
 386: 
 387:     vi.unstubAllEnvs()
 388:   })
 389: })

 126:     const role = roleFromClerk ?? existing?.role ?? 'org:caregiver'
 127: 
 128:     if (existing) {
 129:       await ctx.db.patch(existing._id, {
 130:         role,
 131:         displayName: args.displayName,
 132:         email: args.email,
 133:       })
 134:       if (role === 'org:caregiver') {
 135:         const employeeProfileId = await ensureCaregiverEmployeeProfile(
 136:           ctx,
 137:           tenantId,
 138:           { ...existing, role, displayName: args.displayName, email: args.email },
 139:         )
 140:         if (employeeProfileId) {
 141:           await ctx.scheduler.runAfter(0, internal.adpOutbound.adpSyncWorker, {
 142:             employeeProfileId,
 143:           })
 144:         }
 145:       }
 146:       return existing._id
 147:     }
 148: 
 149:     const memberId = await ctx.db.insert('tenantMembers', {
 150:       tenantId,
 151:       clerkUserId: args.clerkUserId,
 152:       role,
 153:       displayName: args.displayName,
 154:       email: args.email,
 155:     })
 156: 
 157:     if (role === 'org:caregiver') {
 158:       const newMember = await ctx.db.get(memberId)
 159:       if (newMember) {
 160:         const employeeProfileId = await ensureCaregiverEmployeeProfile(
 161:           ctx,
 162:           tenantId,
 163:           newMember,
 164:         )
 165:         if (employeeProfileId) {
 166:           await ctx.scheduler.runAfter(0, internal.adpOutbound.adpSyncWorker, {
 167:             employeeProfileId,
 168:           })
 169:         }
 170:       }
 171:     }
 172: 
 173:     return memberId
 174:   },
 175: })
 176: 
 177: export const updateRole = mutation({
 178:   args: {
 179:     clerkOrgId: v.string(),
 180:     clerkUserId: v.string(),
 181:     role: v.union(
 182:       v.literal('org:admin'),
 183:       v.literal('org:coordinator'),
 184:       v.literal('org:caregiver'),
 185:     ),
 186:   },
 187:   handler: async (ctx, args) => {
 188:     const { tenantId, identity } = await requireTenantRole(ctx, args.clerkOrgId, [
 189:       'org:admin',
 190:     ])
 191: 
 192:     if (args.clerkUserId === identity.subject) {
 193:       throw new Error('Cannot change your own role.')
 194:     }
 195: 
 196:     const existing = await ctx.db
 197:       .query('tenantMembers')
 198:       .withIndex('by_tenant_user', (q) =>
 199:         q.eq('tenantId', tenantId).eq('clerkUserId', args.clerkUserId),
 200:       )
 201:       .unique()
 202: 
 203:     if (!existing) {
 204:       throw new Error('Member not found.')
 205:     }
 206: 
 207:     await ctx.db.patch(existing._id, { role: args.role })
 208: 
 209:     if (args.role === 'org:caregiver') {
 210:       const updatedMember = { ...existing, role: args.role }
 211:       const employeeProfileId = await ensureCaregiverEmployeeProfile(
 212:         ctx,
 213:         tenantId,
 214:         updatedMember,
 215:       )
 216:       if (employeeProfileId) {
 217:         await ctx.scheduler.runAfter(0, internal.adpOutbound.adpSyncWorker, {
 218:           employeeProfileId,
 219:         })
 220:       }
 221:     }
 222: 
 223:     return existing._id
 224:   },
 225: })
 226: 
 227: export const remove = mutation({
 228:   args: {
 229:     clerkOrgId: v.string(),
 230:     clerkUserId: v.string(),
 231:   },
 232:   handler: async (ctx, args) => {
 233:     const { tenantId } = await requireTenantRole(ctx, args.clerkOrgId, [
 234:       'org:admin',
 235:     ])
 236: 
 237:     const existing = await ctx.db
 238:       .query('tenantMembers')
 239:       .withIndex('by_tenant_user', (q) =>
 240:         q.eq('tenantId', tenantId).eq('clerkUserId', args.clerkUserId),
 241:       )
 242:       .unique()
 243: 
 244:     if (existing) {
 245:       await ctx.db.delete(existing._id)
 246:     }
 247:   },
 248: })

Vitest exposes `--configLoader runner`, which avoids bundling the Vite config. I’m running that as a diagnostic only; the required `npm run test` script has already failed, but this may show whether the app tests themselves pass once the config-loader issue is bypassed.
